does it do?</h2><p>DuckDuckBot is a web crawler for <a type="a" url="https://duckduckgo.com/?origin=funnel_help_website" target="_blank" href="https://duckduckgo.com/?origin=funnel_help_website">DuckDuckGo</a>. DuckDuckBot’s job is to constantly improve our search results and offer users the best and most secure search experience possible. The crawler&#x27;s user agent will appear as <code>DuckDuckBot/1.1; (+http://duckduckgo.com/duckduckbot.html)</code>. It respects <a type="a" url="https://metacpan.org/module/WWW::RobotRules" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://metacpan.org/module/WWW::RobotRules">WWW::RobotRules</a> and originates from these IP addresses (also available in JSON at <a type="a" url="https://duckduckgo.com/duckduckbot.json" target="_blank"
